题目选自谢启鸿《复旦大学高等代数习题集》（2023版），§1.5，第27–28页。

记号：\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{19.334}{4.898}{2.423}{%
\RG{0.000}{-0.002}{10.9091}{mi}{110}\RG{5.973}{-0.002}{10.9091}{mi}{59}\RG{10.393}{-0.002}{10.9091}{mi}{109}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 为正整数；\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{44.060}{7.964}{2.991}{%

}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 矩阵，\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{87.094}{7.964}{2.991}{%
\RG{0.000}{-0.002}{10.9091}{mi}{77}\RG{9.657}{-1.496}{7.9701}{mi}{110}\RG{14.800}{-0.002}{10.9091}{rm}{40}\RG{18.672}{-0.002}{10.9091}{mi}{75}\RG{27.838}{-0.002}{10.9091}{rm}{41}\RG{34.567}{-0.002}{10.9091}{rm}{61}\RG{45.179}{-0.002}{10.9091}{mi}{77}\RG{54.837}{-1.496}{7.9701}{mi}{110}\RG{59.525}{-1.496}{7.9701}{sy}{2}\RG{65.536}{-1.496}{7.9701}{mi}{110}\RG{70.679}{-0.002}{10.9091}{rm}{40}\RG{74.550}{-0.002}{10.9091}{mi}{75}\RG{83.717}{-0.002}{10.9091}{rm}{41}%

\section*{2016S10}
(1)证明实对称阵的特征值都是实数，进一步利用Jordan标准型理论和反证法证明实对称阵都可实对角化。

(2)证明实反对称阵的特征值都是\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{5.387}{7.754}{0.730}{%
\RG{0.000}{-0.001}{10.9091}{rm}{48}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 或纯虚数，进一步利用Jordan标准型理论和反证法证明实反对称阵都可复对角化。

证明：(1)设\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{52.428}{8.685}{3.235}{%
\RG{0.000}{0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{65}\RG{11.038}{0.003}{10.9091}{sy}{50}\RG{21.341}{0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{77}\RG{31.675}{-1.633}{7.9701}{mi}{110}\RG{36.818}{0.003}{10.9091}{rm}{40}\RG{40.972}{0.003}{10.9091}{bb}{82}\RG{48.851}{0.003}{10.9091}{rm}{41}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 且\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{36.311}{9.458}{0.500}{%
\RG{0.000}{0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{65}\RG{8.007}{3.961}{7.9701}{rm}{84}\RG{16.770}{0.003}{10.9091}{rm}{61}\RG{28.109}{0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{65}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 。取\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{8.202}{8.292}{0.500}{%
\RG{0.000}{0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{65}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 的任一特征值\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{27.537}{8.183}{0.934}{%
\RG{0.000}{0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{21}\RG{9.261}{0.003}{10.9091}{sy}{50}\RG{19.564}{0.003}{10.9091}{bb}{67}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 及其非零特征向量\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{31.612}{8.183}{0.934}{%
\RG{0.000}{0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{8.479}{0.003}{10.9091}{sy}{50}\RG{18.782}{0.003}{10.9091}{bb}{67}\RG{26.661}{3.961}{7.9701}{mi}{110}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} ，以\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{9.301}{7.844}{0.605}{%
\RG{0.000}{0.004}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{5.449}{3.962}{7.9701}{sy}{3}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 表示共轭转置。由\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{34.897}{8.293}{0.500}{%
\RG{0.000}{0.004}{10.9091}{mi}{65}\RG{8.007}{3.962}{7.9701}{sy}{3}\RG{15.356}{0.004}{10.9091}{rm}{61}\RG{26.695}{0.004}{10.9091}{mi}{65}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} ，有

\[
\RB{143.254}{8.679}{3.241}{%
\RG{11.679}{4.501}{7.9701}{sy}{3}\RG{41.263}{4.501}{7.9701}{sy}{3}\RG{95.170}{4.501}{7.9701}{sy}{3}\RG{130.985}{4.501}{7.9701}{sy}{3}\RG{0.000}{-0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{21}\RG{6.229}{-0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{15.997}{-0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{24.476}{-0.003}{10.9091}{rm}{61}\RG{35.815}{-0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{45.582}{-0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{65}\RG{53.589}{-0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{62.067}{-0.003}{10.9091}{rm}{61}\RG{73.402}{-0.003}{10.9091}{rm}{40}\RG{77.560}{-0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{65}\RG{85.567}{-0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{91.016}{-0.003}{10.9091}{rm}{41}\RG{99.489}{-0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{107.967}{-0.003}{10.9091}{rm}{61}\RG{119.306}{-0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{21}\RG{125.535}{-0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{135.303}{-0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{140.747}{-0.003}{10.9091}{mi}{58}\RR{119.306}{8.846}{6.231}{0.364}%
}
\]
因为\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{34.970}{7.841}{0.805}{%
\RG{0.000}{0.001}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{5.448}{3.959}{7.9701}{sy}{3}\RG{9.767}{0.001}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{18.243}{0.001}{10.9091}{mi}{62}\RG{29.583}{0.001}{10.9091}{rm}{48}%

于是\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{8.202}{8.293}{0.500}{%
\RG{0.000}{0.004}{10.9091}{mi}{65}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 的特征多项式在\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{8.169}{7.976}{0.500}{%
\RG{0.000}{0.004}{10.9091}{bb}{82}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 上完全分裂，可在\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{8.169}{7.976}{0.500}{%
\RG{0.000}{0.004}{10.9091}{bb}{82}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 上取Jordan标准型。若\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{8.202}{8.293}{0.500}{%
\RG{0.000}{0.004}{10.9091}{mi}{65}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 不能实对角化，则其中有大小至少为\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{5.300}{7.759}{0.500}{%
\RG{0.000}{0.005}{10.9091}{rm}{50}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 的Jordan块，因而存在\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{27.734}{8.076}{0.932}{%
\RG{0.000}{0.005}{10.9091}{mi}{21}\RG{9.262}{0.005}{10.9091}{sy}{50}\RG{19.565}{0.005}{10.9091}{bb}{82}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 及\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{42.619}{7.977}{2.721}{%
\RG{0.000}{0.005}{10.9091}{mi}{120}\RG{6.066}{0.005}{10.9091}{mi}{59}\RG{10.844}{0.005}{10.9091}{mi}{121}\RG{19.486}{0.005}{10.9091}{sy}{50}\RG{29.789}{0.005}{10.9091}{bb}{82}\RG{37.668}{3.963}{7.9701}{mi}{110}%

\[

}

(2)设\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{52.428}{8.680}{3.240}{%
\RG{0.000}{-0.002}{10.9091}{mi}{65}\RG{11.039}{-0.002}{10.9091}{sy}{50}\RG{21.342}{-0.002}{10.9091}{mi}{77}\RG{31.675}{-1.638}{7.9701}{mi}{110}\RG{36.819}{-0.002}{10.9091}{rm}{40}\RG{40.973}{-0.002}{10.9091}{bb}{82}\RG{48.851}{-0.002}{10.9091}{rm}{41}%
}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 且\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{47.715}{9.453}{20.968}{%
\RG{0.000}{-0.002}{10.9091}{mi}{65}\RG{8.007}{3.957}{7.9701}{rm}{84}\RG{16.770}{-0.002}{10.9091}{rm}{61}\RG{28.109}{-0.002}{10.9091}{sy}{0}\RG{32.183}{-20.348}{10.9091}{mi}{21}\RG{36.594}{-0.002}{10.9091}{mi}{65}\RG{38.412}{-20.348}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{43.862}{-15.843}{7.9701}{sy}{3}\RR{32.183}{-11.499}{6.231}{0.364}%

\RG{0.000}{-20.348}{10.9091}{rm}{61}\RG{11.335}{-20.348}{10.9091}{rm}{40}\RG{13.395}{-0.002}{10.9091}{mi}{65}\RG{15.493}{-20.348}{10.9091}{mi}{65}\RG{21.402}{-0.002}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{23.500}{-20.348}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{28.949}{-20.348}{10.9091}{rm}{41}\RG{29.881}{-0.002}{10.9091}{rm}{61}\RG{33.103}{-15.843}{7.9701}{sy}{3}\RG{37.421}{-20.348}{10.9091}{mi}{122}\RG{41.219}{-0.002}{10.9091}{mi}{21}\RG{45.899}{-20.348}{10.9091}{rm}{61}\RG{47.448}{-0.002}{10.9091}{mi}{122}%

}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} 其\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{8.202}{0.500}{20.848}{%

\RG{0.000}{-0.004}{10.9091}{mi}{65}\RG{12.018}{-0.004}{10.9091}{sy}{24}\RG{24.514}{-0.004}{10.9091}{mi}{65}\RG{32.521}{3.955}{7.9701}{sy}{0}\RG{38.532}{3.955}{7.9701}{rm}{49}%

}\hskip.1em\allowbreak{} ，所以这正是上述分类；反之，上述各块求逆后仅需分别作相似变换并重排各块，即恢复原标准型。\allowbreak{}\hskip.1em \RB{8.366}{7.992}{0.503}{%
